# Codebase cleanup inventory

Historical source columns below describe past moves. Current semantic paths use
`optimization/semantic_buckets/`; the former root alias has been retired.

Implemented 2026-10-07 in the authoritative TxAgent checkout. This inventory describes changes from the working tree at the start of implementation, including existing uncommitted work; it is not a comparison against a clean Git HEAD.

The cleanup removes **507 paths**, relocates **166 live code, policy and test files**, and preserves **577 historical assets, fixtures, policies and instruction snapshots**. The two move counts overlap for policies and fixtures. Generated Python bytecode is excluded from these counts.

## Resulting ownership

| Folder | Responsibility |
| --- | --- |
| `predict/` | FINAL flat inference, native query priors, transport, retrieval, baselines and traces |
| `data/processing/` | Scientific gold-label and evidence-library construction/publication |
| `optimization/` | Validation-selected direct/mixed context selection |
| `optimization/semantic_buckets/` | Reviewed semantic grouping and scoring |
| `analysis/` | Derived result comparisons |
| `tools/service/` | Deterministic chemistry service |
| `archive/` | Frozen prompts, configurations, reports and ordinary instruction snapshots |
| `docs/` | Cleanup inventory and selected FINAL results |

## Behavioral changes

- New inference uses the FINAL flat workflow. Direct-only and mixed contexts share preparation, scheduling, retries, checkpointing and publication of per-query outputs. Public flat and matrix defaults use `final-full-flat` and the pinned OpenRouter automatic-provider JSON with 128 launcher-local slots. An explicitly selected provider JSON overrides that default; model and route provenance remain part of the new run identity.
- RL/LoRA experiments, progressive execution, old direct/full/group/final-only/coverage workflows, duplicated benchmark implementations, compatibility executable wrappers and obsolete launchers are removed.
- The native BBB, Oral and Skin pipelines retain task-owned prior messages and trace serialization. Ames, DILI and Carcinogens priors retain their pinned messages. Query-only batches complete after their prior and report completion counts with null prediction metrics.
- Ordered selected UIDs, per-level budgets, input identities, immutable request replay, provider provenance and existing trace/result schemas remain authoritative. Changed prompt provenance rejects a resume before overwriting a batch manifest.
- A final result and its trace publish together; a failed trace write cannot leave a successful final checkpoint.
- Historical renderers remain for audit and frozen replay. Archived prompts require an explicit frozen request or pinned Skin successor replay. Retired executable namespaces have no forwarding modules.
- Shared unit conversion, purity classification, index construction, Oral benchmark helpers and BBB prediction filtering now live with their canonical owners. Caller imports, monkeypatch targets and source fingerprint inputs follow those paths.
- Data/publication SDK creation uses `predict/llm_api.py` and the shared provider configuration. Environment credentials take precedence; the existing key-file fallback remains available without copying secrets.
- Existing record-selection optimizers, scientific V7/V8/V9/V10 construction, baselines, semantic code and chemistry services remain live.

## Guidance

Eight active `AGENTS.md` files contain 60 lines in total. The root file contains 21 lines. Selected DeepSeek/Luna tables and their scientific caveats live in [results.md](results.md); the finalizing skill links to that document.

| Active instruction file | Lines |
| --- | --- |
| `AGENTS.md` | 21 |
| `predict/AGENTS.md` | 6 |
| `data/AGENTS.md` | 6 |
| `optimization/AGENTS.md` | 6 |
| `optimization/semantic_buckets/AGENTS.md` | 5 |
| `tools/service/AGENTS.md` | 5 |
| `predict/retrieval/assay_reranking/AGENTS.md` | 6 |
| `archive/AGENTS.md` | 5 |

Retired instructions are ordinary `INSTRUCTIONS.md` snapshots under `archive/instructions/`. Cache-local instructions were preserved in their adjacent README files. `CLAUDE.md` remains a symlink to the root `AGENTS.md`.

## Asset preservation and verification

[archive/RELOCATION.json](../archive/RELOCATION.json) enumerates all 577 preserved assets with original path, current path and SHA-256 or symlink target. Ordinary instruction snapshots explicitly have no legacy alias. All recorded hashes, aliases and symlink targets were verified.

- The FINAL `collection.json` is byte-for-byte unchanged. It still indexes 61 complete test arms with 19,899 successful query-arm results.
- Completed runs, release payloads, frozen implementation snapshots, CURRENT pointers and the FINAL Git bundle were not regenerated. No paid model calls, commit, push or bundle recompression was performed.
- 186 saved prompt-message and reference-index comparisons, including six-task prior messages, match the original rendering exactly.
- The frozen Joseph comparison renderer rebuilds PNG/PDF/SVG and verifies its 26-row CSV and four-curve receipt. Original renderer and layout hashes still validate; its JSON helper import now follows the canonical module.
- 1163 retained Python files parse successfully; imports do not reference retired `tools.chembl_tool`, progressive or root-baseline executables.
- The initial binary Git diff and small source backup, original-source comparison checkout and validation logs are retained under `/tmp/txagent-cleanup-20261007/` for local review/recovery. Published experiment payloads were not copied into that temporary backup.

## Validation results

The suites below were run offline. Optional baseline dependencies were supplied from their existing project environments; HTTP/SDK/pytest packages needed by those environments were installed only under the temporary validation folder.

| Check | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Optimization and shared credential tests | 60 passed |
| Baselines | 49 passed |
| FINAL prompt/matrix and six-task preparation/provenance checks | 108 passed |
| FINAL launch, stage runtime, replay, provenance, import boundaries and live-run checks after final changes | 142 passed |
| Data layout, benchmark contracts, transport and V7/V8/V9/V10 publication tests | 702 passed; 8 existing failures; 1,074 subtests passed |
| Provider override forwarding | 2 passed |
| Relocated global scheduler tests | 8 passed |
| Exact rendering comparison | 186 unchanged cases |
| Frozen comparison plot | Rebuilt with identical CSV rows |

The broader inference/evidence/semantic run and the existing failure inventory are recorded below. Existing failures were replayed against the source saved before this cleanup; refactor-related fixture/CLI issues were corrected rather than excluded.

The final broad inference/evidence/semantic run reported **2,261 passed, 52 existing failures and 2 skipped**. Every failing test and short-ID subtest was reproduced with the original source; the failing-test sets match. A later focused check after aligning provider defaults passed **142 tests**, and the relocated global scheduler checks passed **8 tests**.

### Existing failures confirmed with original sources

The inference/evidence/semantic comparison reproduced 46 failing tests plus six failing short-ID subtests. The publication-contract comparison reproduced eight additional failing tests. They include old parent-normalizer expectations, missing frozen scientific assets/mappings, pinned source-digest drift, stale Stage 1/Stage 2 expectations, relative Oral value parsing, mocked provider response/provenance assumptions, function-length enforcement, and historical trace-viewer discovery. Scientific inputs and contracts were not altered to make these assertions pass.

- `data/processing/evidence_library/versions/v8/tests/test_bbb_measurement_rules.py::test_stage1_does_not_use_the_categorical_encoder_for_routing`

The final broad offline run omits three environment/artifact-dependent modules:

- `tests/predict/test_ranked_uid_retrieval.py`: optional transformer/tokenizer runtime is unavailable in the default Python environment.
- `tests/data/evidence_library/test_source_uid_levels.py`: depends on a missing release-level mapping/artifact and performs full UID coverage reads.
- `tests/data/evidence_library/tasks/skin_reaction/test_normalized_evidence_library.py`: setup is blocked by pinned raw-source digest drift.


## Root clutter follow-up (2026-10-07)

Removed four root directories containing only generated caches or stale pytest build locks. The legacy baseline source was already relocated or retired in the inventory above. The canonical-builder boundary tests now write their build locks beneath pytest `tmp_path`, preventing recreation of `canonical-root/` in the checkout. Ruff and Python caches can be recreated by later tool runs.

`dili_score/` remains an active library imported by `optimization/dili_score_cache.py`; its source and scientific cache identities are preserved. `.scratch/`, `.tmp/`, `artifacts/`, `tables/` and `traces/` contain scientific or historical material and were preserved.

The two dirty, stale build-lock files were backed up byte-for-byte to `/vast/projects/myatskar/design-documents/joseph/.codex/backups/txagent-stale-build-locks-20261007T223914Z.tar.gz` before deletion. Generated bytecode and Ruff caches were discarded. No scientific workflow was launched.

## Folder ownership follow-up (2026-10-07)

Implementation inventory recorded before relocation. Optimization owns offline DILI scoring and the complete semantic-bucket lifecycle. `analysis/` remains independent. The root `semantic_buckets` path becomes an asset compatibility symlink to `optimization/semantic_buckets/`; live imports, module commands and filesystem references use the canonical owner. Frozen manifests, policies, prompts, provenance and historical relocation records retain their bytes.

Scoped source backup and preservation metadata: `/tmp/txagent-folder-organization-20261007-6sv_mxxw`. No scientific workflow is regenerated or launched.

### Pytest cache generated during validation

Pytest cache writes now use `/tmp/txagent-pytest-cache`. The initially generated root cache was moved outside the checkout:

- `.pytest_cache/.gitignore` → `/tmp/txagent-pytest-cache/.gitignore`
- `.pytest_cache/CACHEDIR.TAG` → `/tmp/txagent-pytest-cache/CACHEDIR.TAG`
- `.pytest_cache/README.md` → `/tmp/txagent-pytest-cache/README.md`
- `.pytest_cache/v/cache/lastfailed` → `/tmp/txagent-pytest-cache/v/cache/lastfailed`
- `.pytest_cache/v/cache/nodeids` → `/tmp/txagent-pytest-cache/v/cache/nodeids`

Created parent directories:
- `docs/paper/`

### Every live file edited or added in this follow-up

Moves above retain their existing bytes except the live source and guidance edits listed here. Import prefixes, module commands and repository-root calculations follow the new optimization owner. Scientific payload ignore rules now follow that owner too. AMES scoring and publication accept both frozen historical and canonical input-path keys while still rejecting changed weight hashes. DILI derived reports continue to resolve under the repository `outputs/analysis/` owner.
`pyproject.toml` builds only the four-file public `dili_score` package, keeps source tests discoverable without a manual PYTHONPATH, collects semantic tests once through their canonical owner, and puts pytest cache writes in `/tmp`. The new cache test verifies scorer-version and source-hash rejection using tiny synthetic inputs. No rule version, provider identity, selected score, frozen receipt or manifest is rewritten.

The first system-Python commands lacked optional apricot, torch, molgpka or transformers dependencies. The affected tests passed in existing project environments. The system-Python functional-group failure also reproduces with the saved original test; the complete chemistry-service suite passes in its existing environment. No shared environment was modified.

The production-artifact `test_weighted_policy_v2.py` audit was not run because it hashes large frozen datasets. The existing function-length guard was also excluded from the behavioral suite: an AST comparison with the saved original source confirms the same three violations (63, 63 and 61 lines). These are existing limitations, unchanged by this relocation.

This follow-up relocates 419 existing directories and 2,416 files, edits 121 existing live files and adds two files. It launches no scientific batch and changes no scientific selection. Detailed temporary receipts remain in `/tmp/txagent-folder-organization-20261007-6sv_mxxw`; this inventory preserves the durable summary. Existing unrelated working-tree changes remain in place.

## Pinned Skin measurement profile follow-up (2026-10-11)

The historical Skin `measurement_resolution_v1/measurement_resolution.manifest.json` pins an endpoint/unit profile containing 117 source/endpoint entries. Its summaries and examples were rendered into the original measurement-extraction prompts. The current V10 Skin renderer ignores endpoint-profile blocks; this snapshot is retained for historical prompt reconstruction and provenance.

- Relocated `.scratch/skin_endpoint_unit_profile_v3.json` to `data/artifacts/evidence_library_assets/skin_reaction/construction_assets/measurement_resolution_v1/skin_endpoint_unit_profile_v3.json`.
- Replaced the original scratch file with a relative compatibility symlink to `../data/artifacts/evidence_library_assets/skin_reaction/construction_assets/measurement_resolution_v1/skin_endpoint_unit_profile_v3.json` so the frozen manifest's path still resolves.

Verified the relocated snapshot and compatibility path against the manifest's SHA-256, `98bfd531896e4587df9a223de01d2a60b887f95a7d7c10e2015495c5c23e03aa`. The rename preserved inode and modification time. The existing measurement manifest and neighboring `endpoint_unit_profile.json` retain their bytes; the latter is a different snapshot and cannot substitute for this one. No data or prompt was regenerated.

## Scratch directory removal (2026-10-12)

Removed the repository-root `.scratch/` directory after preserving all 1,960 regular files, nine symlinks and 227 directories in `archive/workspace_history/20261012T043138Z/scratch.tar.gz`. The archive is 1,581,328,502 bytes and its SHA-256 is `430e58b6bb28a92d9d7e4782c743be87af53ab9b0226b5ecc772184a95b67d06`.

Every original path, type and link target is enumerated in the [scratch inventory](../archive/workspace_history/20261012T043138Z/scratch.inventory.json). The [verification receipt](../archive/workspace_history/20261012T043138Z/scratch.verification.json) records complete archive coverage, a successful byte-for-byte `tar --gzip --compare`, unchanged source stat fingerprints before removal and no active current-host process use. No nested mount existed under `.scratch/`, and deletion did not follow external symlinks.

The Skin endpoint profile remains in its historical construction-assets owner with its original pinned hash. The scratch compatibility link was archived with the rest of the directory. Historical replay or verification that reads the frozen manifest's original scratch path must restore that link first; [restore instructions](../archive/workspace_history/20261012T043138Z/README.md) cover the whole directory and the single input path. The frozen Skin, BBB and Oral manifests retain their bytes.

The workspace-history tarball is excluded from Git; its compact inventory, checksums and restore instructions remain trackable. This entry covers `.scratch/` only.
